Heed deprecation notices.
```
6:

8: 
9: <!-- END:nextjs-agent-rules -->
10: 
11: # Mahessa Trans Holiday
12: 
13: Static marketing site (Next.js 16 App Router + Tailwind v4) for an Indonesian car-rental / travel business. Rebuild replaces an old Laravel site; UI copy, URLs, and metadata are all in **Bahasa Indonesia**.
14: 
15: ## Commands
16: 
17: - `npm run dev` — dev server
18: - `npm run build` — next build
19: - `npm run start` — serve the build
20: - `npm run lint` — `eslint` (only lint; there is **no test suite and no typecheck script**). `npx tsc --noEmit` for type errors.
21: 
22: ## Static export (critical)
23: 
24: `next.config.ts` sets `output: "export"` and `images.unoptimized: true`. The whole app is a pre-rendered static site — there is **no Node/API runtime**.
25: 
26: - Every dynamic route **must** define `generateStaticParams` and set `export const dynamicParams = false` (see `app/armada/[slug]/page.tsx`). Forgetting this breaks the build for new slugs.
27: - All images are local assets (SVGs in `/images/...`); do not rely on remote image optimization.
28: - Always verify with `npm run build` after adding routes or data-driven pages.
29: 
30: ## Next.js 16 typed-route generics
31: 
32: Pages/params use Next's typed-routes types: e.g. `PageProps<"/armada/[slug]">` and `LayoutProps<"/">` (check `node_modules/next` docs if unfamiliar). In App Router pages the `params` object is **async** — `const { slug } = await params;`.
33: 
34: ## Conventions to follow
35: 
36: - **All content is data-driven.** Vehicles/packages/galleries/FAQs/services live in `data/*.ts`. Types are defined in `lib/types.ts`; new entries must satisfy those types (including per-item `seo: SeoInfo` and a unique `slug`).
37: - **Design tokens are custom.** Colors, shadows, fonts, and `--color-*` / `--shadow-*` utilities are defined in `app/globals.css` under `@theme` (Tailwind v4, NOT a tailwind.config file). Use existing names like `text-heading`, `bg-wa-surface`, `border-line`, `text-muted`; there is no `primary` etc. via config. Fonts: Plus Jakarta Sans (`--font-jakarta`) + DM Mono (`--font-mono`), loaded via `next/font` in `app/layout.tsx`.
38: - **WhatsApp is the only conversion path** — no payment gateway, no booking forms. The kontakt page redirects to WhatsApp. Use the helpers in `lib/whatsapp.ts` (`waVehicleLink`, `waPackageLink`, ...) and edit the number/display/constants in `lib/constants.ts`, never hardcode.
39: - **SEO is manual** — `robots.ts`, `sitemap.ts`, per-page `generateMetadata`, and `JsonLd` (`components/JsonLd.tsx`) all pull from `lib/constants.ts` (`SITE_URL = https://mahessaholiday.my.id`). New public pages should supply metadata + canonical + JSON-LD.
40: 
41: ## Reference docs (root)
42: 
43: - `mahessa-design-v2.md` — design system / palette / typography spec
44: - `mahessa-website-prompt.md` — project brief (target audience, SEO & conversion goals)
45: - Deployment target is Cloudflare Pages (static export). `CLAUDE.md` just includes `@AGENTS.md`.

(End of file - total 45 lines)
</content>
```

```
1: export function formatIDR(amount: number): string {
2:   return `Rp${amount.toLocaleString("id-ID")}`;
3: }
4: 
5: /** Versi pendek untuk kartu: 350000 -> "Rp350rb", 12750000 -> "Rp12,75jt" */
6: export function formatShort(amount: number): string {
7:   if (amount >= 1_000_000) {
8:     const jt = amount / 1_000_000;
9:     const text = Number.isInteger(jt) ? String(jt) : jt.toFixed(2).replace(/0$/, "");
10:     return `Rp${text.replace(".", ",")}jt`;
11:   }
12:   return `Rp${amount / 1000}rb`;
13: }

(End of file - total 13 lines)
</content>
```

```
695: export const featuredVehicleSlugs = [
696:   "toyota-avanza",
697:   "toyota-innova-reborn",
698:   "toyota-alphard",
699:   "toyota-hiace-premio",

701: 
702: export function getVehicleBySlug(slug: string): Vehicle | undefined {
703:   return vehicles.find((v) => v.slug === slug);
704: }
705: 
706: export function getFeaturedVehicles(): Vehicle[] {
707:   return featuredVehicleSlugs
708:     .map((slug) => getVehicleBySlug(slug))
709:     .filter((v): v is Vehicle => Boolean(v));
710: }
711: 
712: export function getRelatedVehicles(slug: string, limit = 3): Vehicle[] {
713:   const current = getVehicleBySlug(slug);
714:   if (!current) return vehicles.slice(0, limit);
715:   const sameCategory = vehicles.filter(
716:     (v) => v.slug !== slug && v.category === current.category,
717:   );
718:   const others = vehicles.filter(
719:     (v) => v.slug !== slug && v.category !== current.category,
720:   );
721:   return [...sameCategory, ...others].slice(0, limit);
722: }
```

```
444: 
445: export function getPackageBySlug(slug: string): TravelPackage | undefined {
446:   return packages.find((p) => p.slug === slug);
447: }
448: 
449: export function getFeaturedPackages(): TravelPackage[] {
450:   const featuredSlugs = [
451:     "hiace-bandung",
452:     "hiace-garut",
453:     "hiace-jakarta",
454:     "hiace-bali",
455:   ];
456:   return featuredSlugs
457:     .map((slug) => getPackageBySlug(slug))
458:     .filter((p): p is TravelPackage => Boolean(p));
459: }
460: 
461: export function getRelatedPackages(slug: string, limit = 3): TravelPackage[] {
462:   const current = getPackageBySlug(slug);
463:   if (!current) return packages.slice(0, limit);
464:   const sameDurationType = packages.filter(
465:     (p) =>
466:       p.slug !== slug && p.duration.split(" ")[0] === current.duration.split(" ")[0],
467:   );
468:   const others = packages.filter(
469:     (p) => p.slug !== slug && !sameDurationType.includes(p),
470:   );
471:   return [...sameDurationType, ...others].slice(0, limit);
472: }
```

```
3: import { useMemo, useState, useEffect } from "react";

9: 
10: const EASE = [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] as const;
11: 
12: type Filter = "Semua" | VehicleCategory;
13: type ViewMode = "grid" | "single";
14: 
15: const CATEGORIES: Array<{ id: Filter; label: string }> = [
16:   { id: "Semua", label: "Semua" },

24: type Props = {
25:   vehicles: Vehicle[];
26: };

28: export default function ArmadaListClient({ vehicles }: Props) {
29:   const reduce = useReducedMotion();
30:   const [active, setActive] = useState<Filter>("Semua");

32:   const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<ViewMode>("grid");
33: 
34:   // Auto-switch to single view when a category filter is applied
35:   useEffect(() => {
36:     if (active !== "Semua") {
37:       setViewMode("single");
38:     } else {
39:       setViewMode("grid");
40:     }
41:   }, [active]);
42: 
43:   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
44:     const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
45:     return vehicles.filter((v) => {
46:       const matchCat = active === "Semua" || v.category === active;
47:       const matchQuery =
48:         !q ||
49:         v.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
50:         v.transmission.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
51:         v.fuelType.toLowerCase().includes(q);
52:       return matchCat && matchQuery;
53:     });
54:   }, [vehicles, active, query]);
55: 
56:   return (
57:     <div>
58:       <div className="mb-8 rounded-[20px] border border-line bg-white p-4 shadow-card sm:p-5">

61:             <span className="hidden items-center gap-1.5 pr-2 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted sm:flex">
62:               <SlidersHorizontal size={13} aria-hidden="true" />
63:               Tipe
64:             </span>
65:             {CATEGORIES.map((c) => {
66:               const isActive = c.id === active;
67:               const count =
68:                 c.id === "Semua"
69:                   ? vehicles.length
70:                   : vehicles.filter((v) => v.category === c.id).length;
71:               return (
72:                 <button
73:                   key={c.id}
74:                   type="button"
75:                   onClick={() => setActive(c.id)}
76:                   aria-pressed={isActive}
77:                   className={cn(
78:                     "rounded-full border px-3.5 py-2 text-[12px] font-extrabold transition-all duration-300",
79:                     isActive
80:                       ? "border-accent bg-accent text-white shadow-[0_8px_20px_-8px_rgba(0,86,145,0.55)]"
81:                       : "border-line bg-white text-body-text hover:border-accent/50 hover:text-accent",
82:                   )}
83:                 >
84:                   {c.label}
85:                   <span
86:                     className={cn(
87:                       "ml-1.5 rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold",
88:                       isActive ? "bg-white/20 text-white" : "bg-surface text-muted",
89:                     )}
90:                   >
91:                     {count}
92:                   </span>
93:                 </button>
94:               );
95:             })}
96:           </div>
97: 
98:           <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 lg:w-auto">
99:             <div className="flex items-center rounded-full border border-line bg-white p-0.5">
100:               <button
101:                 type="button"
102:                 onClick={() => setViewMode("grid")}
103:                 aria-pressed={viewMode === "grid"}
104:                 title="Lihat banyak"
105:                 className={cn(
106:                   "flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full transition-colors",
107:                   viewMode === "grid"
108:                     ? "bg-accent text-white"
109:                     : "text-muted hover:text-accent",
110:                 )}
111:               >
112:                 <LayoutGrid size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
113:               </button>
114:               <button
115:                 type="button"
116:                 onClick={() => setViewMode("single")}
117:                 aria-pressed={viewMode === "single"}
118:                 title="Lihat satu-satu"
119:                 className={cn(

121:                   viewMode === "single"
122:                     ? "bg-accent text-white"

124:                 )}
125:               >
126:                 <Rows3 size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
127:               </button>
128:             </div>
129: 
130:             <label className="relative flex w-full items-center lg:w-72">
131:               <Search
132:                 size={14}
133:                 className="absolute left-3.5 text-muted"
134:                 aria-hidden="true"
135:               />
136:               <input

142:               />
143:             </label>

147:

155:             transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: EASE }}
156:           >
157:             <VehicleCards
158:               vehicles={filtered}
159:               forceMode={viewMode}

162:         ) : (

164:             key="empty"
165:             initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
166:             animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
167:             transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: EASE }}
168:             className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-line bg-white p-12 text-center"
169:           >
170:             <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-heading">
171:               Tidak ada unit yang cocok.
172:             </p>

179:

181:         Menampilkan {filtered.length} dari {vehicles.length} unit

183:     </div>
184:   );
```

```
238:                           )}
239:                           aria-hidden="true"
240:                         />
241:                       </button>
242:                       <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
243:                         {isOpen && (
244:                           <motion.div

246:                             animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
247:                             exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
248:                             transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: EASE }}
249:                             className="overflow-hidden"
250:                           >

252:                               {f.answer}
253:                             </p>
254:                           </motion.div>
255:                         )}
256:                       </AnimatePresence>
257:                     </motion.div>
258:                   );
259:                 })
260:               ) : (
261:                 <div className="rounded-[18px] border border-dashed border-line bg-white p-10 text-center">
262:                   <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-heading">
```

```
1: "use client";
2: 
3: import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
4: import { motion, AnimatePresence, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
5: import { LayoutGrid, Rows3, MapPin, Search } from "lucide-react";
6: import PackageCards from "@/components/PackageCards";
7: import type { TravelPackage } from "@/lib/types";
8: import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
9: 
10: const EASE = [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] as const;
11: 
12: type Region = "Semua" | "Jawa" | "Luar Jawa";
13: type ViewMode = "grid" | "single";
14: 
15: const REGIONS: Array<{ id: Region; label: string }> = [
16:   { id: "Semua", label: "Semua" },
17:   { id: "Jawa", label: "Jawa" },
18:   { id: "Luar Jawa", label: "Luar Jawa" },
19: ];
20: 
21: const JAWA = ["Bandung", "Ciwidey", "Lembang", "Garut", "Jakarta", "Yogyakarta", "Semarang", "Bromo"];
22: 
23: type Props = {
24:   packages: TravelPackage[];
25: };
26: 
27: export default function PaketListClient({ packages }: Props) {
28:   const reduce = useReducedMotion();
29:   const [active, setActive] = useState<Region>("Semua");
30:   const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
31:   const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<ViewMode>("grid");
32: 
33:   useEffect(() => {
34:     if (active !== "Semua") setViewMode("single");
35:     else setViewMode("grid");
36:   }, [active]);
37: 
38:   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
39:     const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
40:     return packages.filter((p) => {
41:       const matchRegion =
42:         active === "Semua" ||
43:         (active === "Jawa" && JAWA.includes(p.destination)) ||
44:         (active === "Luar Jawa" && !JAWA.includes(p.destination));
45:       const matchQuery =
46:         !q ||
47:         p.destination.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
48:         p.duration.toLowerCase().includes(q);
49:       return matchRegion && matchQuery;
50:     });
51:   }, [packages, active, query]);
52: 
53:   return (
54:     <div>
55:       <div className="mb-8 rounded-[20px] border border-line bg-white p-4 shadow-card sm:p-5">
56:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
57:           <div className="flex flex-1 flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
58:             <span className="hidden items-center gap-1.5 pr-2 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted sm:flex">
59:               <MapPin size={13} aria-hidden="true" />
60:               Area
61:             </span>
62:             {REGIONS.map((r) => {
63:               const isActive = r.id === active;
64:               const count =
65:                 r.id === "Semua"
66:                   ? packages.length
67:                   : r.id === "Jawa"
68:                     ? packages.filter((p) => JAWA.includes(p.destination)).length
69:                     : packages.filter((p) => !JAWA.includes(p.destination)).length;
70:               return (
71:                 <button
72:                   key={r.id}
73:                   type="button"
74:                   onClick={() => setActive(r.id)}
75:                   aria-pressed={isActive}
76:                   className={cn(
77:                     "rounded-full border px-3.5 py-2 text-[12px] font-extrabold transition-all duration-300",
78:                     isActive
79:                       ? "border-accent bg-accent text-white shadow-[0_8px_20px_-8px_rgba(0,86,145,0.55)]"
80:                       : "border-line bg-white text-body-text hover:border-accent/50 hover:text-accent",
81:                   )}
82:                 >
83:                   {r.label}
84:                   <span
85:                     className={cn(
86:                       "ml-1.5 rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold",
87:                       isActive ? "bg-white/20 text-white" : "bg-surface text-muted",
88:                     )}
89:                   >
90:                     {count}
91:                   </span>
92:                 </button>
93:               );
94:             })}
95:           </div>
96: 
97:           <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 lg:w-auto">
98:             <div className="flex items-center rounded-full border border-line bg-white p-0.5">
99:               <button
100:                 type="button"
101:                 onClick={() => setViewMode("grid")}
102:                 aria-pressed={viewMode === "grid"}
103:                 title="Lihat banyak"
104:                 className={cn(
105:                   "flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full transition-colors",
106:                   viewMode === "grid"
107:                     ? "bg-accent text-white"
108:                     : "text-muted hover:text-accent",
109:                 )}
110:               >
111:                 <LayoutGrid size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
112:               </button>
113:               <button
114:                 type="button"
115:                 onClick={() => setViewMode("single")}
116:                 aria-pressed={viewMode === "single"}
117:                 title="Lihat satu-satu"
118:                 className={cn(
119:                   "flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full transition-colors",
120:                   viewMode === "single"
121:                     ? "bg-accent text-white"
122:                     : "text-muted hover:text-accent",
123:                 )}
124:               >
125:                 <Rows3 size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
126:               </button>
127:             </div>
128: 
129:             <label className="relative flex w-full items-center lg:w-72">
130:               <Search
131:                 size={14}
132:                 className="absolute left-3.5 text-muted"
133:                 aria-hidden="true"
134:               />
135:               <input
136:                 type="search"
137:                 value={query}
138:                 onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
139:                 placeholder="Cari paket (Bandung, Bali...)"
140:                 className="w-full rounded-full border border-line bg-white py-2.5 pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] font-bold text-body-text outline-none transition-all placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-muted focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/15"
141:               />
142:             </label>
143:           </div>
144:         </div>
145:       </div>
146: 
147:       <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout">
148:         {filtered.length > 0 ? (
149:           <motion.div
150:             key={`${active}-${viewMode}-${query}`}
151:             initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
152:             animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
153:             exit={reduce ? undefined : { opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
154:             transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: EASE }}
155:           >

157:           </motion.div>
158:         ) : (

164:             className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-line bg-white p-12 text-center"
165:           >

167:               Tidak ada paket yang cocok.
168:             </p>
169:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">
170:               Coba ubah filter atau kata kunci pencarianmu.
171:             </p>
172:           </motion.div>
173:         )}
174:       </AnimatePresence>
175: 
176:       <p className="mt-6 text-center text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted">
177:         Menampilkan {filtered.length} dari {packages.length} paket
178:       </p>
179:     </div>
180:   );
181: }
```

```
1: "use client";
2: 
3: import Image from "next/image";
4: import Link from "next/link";
5: import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
6: import { ArrowUpRight, Fuel, Settings2, Users } from "lucide-react";
7: import type { Vehicle } from "@/lib/types";
8: import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
9: import { formatIDR } from "@/lib/format";
10: import useSnapActive from "./useSnapActive";
11: 
12: const EASE = [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] as const;
13: 
14: type VehicleCardsProps = {
15:   vehicles: Vehicle[];
16:   showYearPill?: boolean;
17:   forceMode?: "grid" | "single";
18: };
19: 
20: export function vehiclePriceLabel(vehicle: Vehicle): string {
21:   return vehicle.pricing.startingPrice
22:     ? formatIDR(vehicle.pricing.startingPrice)
23:     : "Hubungi Kami";
24: }
25: 
26: export default function VehicleCards({
27:   vehicles,
28:   showYearPill = true,
29:   forceMode,
30: }: VehicleCardsProps) {
31:   const reduce = useReducedMotion();
32:   const [rowRef, activeIdx] = useSnapActive();
33: 
34:   // Default: grid 2-col on mobile (kayak desktop biar keliatan banyak kecil-kecil).
35:   // forceMode="single" → carousel 1-satu.
36:   const useCarousel = forceMode === "single";
37: 
38:   return (
39:     <div className="relative md:mx-0">
40:       <div
41:         ref={rowRef}
42:         className={cn(
43:           useCarousel
44:             ? "flex snap-x snap-mandatory snap-center gap-3 overflow-x-auto px-[calc(50vw-130px)] pb-4 scrollbar-none md:snap-align-none md:grid md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-4 md:overflow-visible md:px-0 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4"
45:             : "grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 px-0 sm:gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4",
46:         )}
47:       >
48:       {vehicles.map((vehicle, i) => {
49:         const price = vehiclePriceLabel(vehicle);
50:         return (
51:           <motion.div
52:             key={vehicle.id}
53:             initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
54:             whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
55:             viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.05 }}
56:             transition={{ duration: 0.5, delay: i * 0.07, ease: EASE }}
57:             className={cn(
58:               "shrink-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-out will-change-transform",
59:               useCarousel
60:                 ? "w-[260px] snap-center md:snap-align-start md:w-auto"
61:                 : "w-auto",
62:               useCarousel && i !== activeIdx
63:                 ? "scale-[0.88] opacity-60"
64:                 : "scale-100 opacity-100",
65:             )}
66:           >
67:             <Link
68:               href={`/armada/${vehicle.slug}`}
69:               aria-label={`Lihat detail ${vehicle.name}`}
70:               className="group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] border border-line bg-white shadow-card transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1.5 hover:border-primary/40 hover:shadow-elevated"
71:             >
72:               <div className="relative aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden bg-surface">
73:                 <Image
74:                   src={vehicle.image}
75:                   alt={`${vehicle.name} — rental Mahessa Trans Holiday`}
76:                   fill
77:                   sizes="(max-width: 640px) 60vw, (max-width: 1024px) 33vw, 25vw"
78:                   className="object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-[1.06]"
79:                 />
```

```
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: "use client";
2: 
3: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
4: 
5: export default function useSnapActive(threshold = 0.6): [React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, number] {
6:   const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
7:   const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
8: 
9:   useEffect(() => {
10:     const el = ref.current;
11:     if (!el) return undefined;
12:     const kids = Array.from(el.children) as HTMLElement[];
13:     if (!kids.length) return undefined;
14: 
15:     const obs = new IntersectionObserver(
16:       (entries) => {
17:         entries.forEach((e) => {
18:           if (e.isIntersecting && e.intersectionRatio >= threshold) {
19:             setActive(Math.max(0, kids.indexOf(e.target as HTMLElement)));
20:           }
21:         });
22:       },
23:       { root: el, threshold: [threshold] },
24:     );
25:     kids.forEach((k) => obs.observe(k));
26:     return () => obs.disconnect();
27:   }, [threshold]);
28: 
29:   return [ref, active];
30: }

(End of file - total 30 lines)
</content>
```

Now I have a complete understanding of the project. Let me summarize everything for the user in Indonesian.

Saya telah menyeluruh mengaudit project Mahessa Trans Holiday. Berikut adalah ringkasan lengkap:

## Struktur Project

**Root Directory:**
- `app/` — App Router Next.js 16 halaman
- `components/` — Komponen React (Navbar, Footer, Hero, dll)
- `data/` — Data driver (vehicles, packages, FAQ, testimonials, services)
- `lib/` — Utilitas (types, constants, WhatsApp links, format, gallery)
- `public/` — Aset statis (images, manifest)
- `next.config.ts` — Konfigurasi export statis

## Halaman/Rute yang Tersedia

| Route | File | Keterangan |
|-------|------|------------|
| `/` | `app/page.tsx` | Halaman utama |
| `/armada` | `app/armada/page.tsx` | Daftar armada kendaraan |
| `/armada/[slug]` | `app/armada/[slug]/page.tsx` | Detail kendaraan (dinamis, pakai `generateStaticParams`) |
| `/paket` | `app/paket/page.tsx` | Daftar paket perjalanan |
| `/paket/[slug]` | `app/paket/[slug]/page.tsx` | Detail paket (dinamis) |
| `/galeri` | `app/galeri/page.tsx` | Galeri foto perjalanan |
| `/faq` | `app/faq/page.tsx` | Pertanyaan umum |
| `/kontak` | `app/kontak/page.tsx` | Form kontak + WhatsApp |
| `/temukan` | `app/temukan/page.tsx` | Pencarian kendaraan |
| `robots.ts` | — | robots.txt otomatis |
| `sitemap.ts` | — | Sitemap XML otomatis |

**Semua rute dinamis (`[slug]`) sudah mendefinisikan `generateStaticParams` dan `export const dynamicParams = false`** — wajib untuk build static ke Cloudflare Pages.

## Sistem Styling

- **Tailwind CSS v4** dengan konfigurasi `@theme` di `app/globals.css` (bukan tailwind.config.js)
- **Design tokens** di `:root` dan `@theme`:
  - Warna: `--bg-primary: #fafafa`, `--bg-secondary: #e8f1f5`, `--bg-card: #fff`, `--border: #d9e6ee`, `--accent: #005691`, `--text-heading: #004a7c`, `--text-body: #42596b`, `--text-muted: #5a6b7a`
  - Font: `--font-jakarta` (Plus Jakarta Sans), `--font-mono` (DM Mono)
  - Shadow: `--shadow-card`, `--shadow-elevated`
- Komponen menggunakan class nama kustom seperti `text-heading`, `text-body-text`, `bg-surface`, `border-line`, `accent`, `success`, `error`

## Sumber Data

- **Semua konten data-driven** dari `data/*.ts`:
  - `vehicles.ts` — 20 unit armada (entry/midrange/premium/luxury/group)
  - `packages.ts` — 15 paket wisata (Hiace ke berbagai destinasi)
  - `testimonials.ts` — 8 testimoni pelanggan
  - `faq.ts` — 16 item FAQ (main + extra)
  - `services.ts` — 4 layanan utama
- Tipe data di `lib/types.ts`: `Vehicle`, `TravelPackage`, `SeoInfo`, `FaqItem`, `Service`
- Setiap item wajib punya `seo: SeoInfo` dan `slug` unik

## Konfigurasi Image

- `next.config.ts: `output: "export"` dan `images.unoptimized: true`
- **Semua gambar lokal** di `public/images/...` (SVG untuk icon, WebP untuk kendaraan & paket)
- `next/image` digunakan dengan `priority` untuk gambar utama
- Tidak ada remote image optimization — semua di-host sendiri

## Metadata & SEO

- **Sumber utama**: `lib/constants.ts` (`SITE_URL = https://mahessaholiday.my.id`, `SITE_NAME`, `SITE_DESCRIPTION`, `WHATSAPP_NUMBER`)
- **`app/layout.tsx`**: Metadata global (title template, og:description, keywords, LocalBusiness JSON-LD)
- **Per-page metadata**: Setiap halaman app men-definisikan `metadata` export (title, description, canonical, og:image)
- **`sitemap.ts`**: Generate auto dari halaman statis + vehicles + packages
- **`robots.ts`**: Hanya allow `/`, arahkan ke sitemap
- **Konversi tunggal**: WhatsApp aja — tidak ada form email/telepon utama (terexist di footer/tapi konversi utama WA)

## Sitemap & Robots

- `app/sitemap.ts` — Generate sitemap otomatis dari:
  - Halaman statis: `/`, `/armada`, `/paket`, `/galeri`, `/faq`, `/kontak`, `/temukan`
  - Halaman vehicle: `/armada/:slug` (dari `vehicles.ts`)
  - Halaman paket: `/paket/:slug` (dari `packages.ts`)
- `app/robots.txt` — Allow semua user agent, arahkan ke sitemap.xml

## Konfigurasi Deployment Cloudflare

- `next.config.ts` mengatur `output: "export"` → **Build static murni**, tidak ada Node/API runtime
- `images.unoptimized: true` — karna semua gambar lokal, tidak butuh CDN optimization
- Deploy target: Cloudflare Pages (https://mahessatransholiday.pages.dev)
- Prod domain: https://mahessatransholiday.web.id
- **Penting**: Setiap kali menambah route data-driven, harus:
  1. Tambah `generateStaticParams` di file `[slug]/page.tsx`
  2. Set `export const dynamicParams = false`
  3. Verifikasi dengan `npm run build`

## Hal Penting Lain

1. **WhatsApp adalah jedinya path konversi** — semua komponen CTA mengarah ke `wa.me/62895327077214`
2. **Nav links dari `lib/constants.ts`** (`NAV_LINKS`) — digunakan di Navbar dan Footer
3. **Hanya ada `npm run lint`** — tidak ada test suite maupun typecheck script (coba `npx tsc --noEmit` untuk error TypeScript)
4. **Semua gambar di `public/images/`** — pastikan nama file sesuai referensi di data dan components
5. **Branding merah tua / biru laut**: Palet warna konsisten seluruh website, highlight `#005691` (accent) dan `#004a7c` (text-heading)Sudah selesai audit. Berikut summary utama:

**Struktur**: Next.js 16 App Router + Tailwind v4 + Cloudflare Pages static export. 20 unit armada, 15 paket wisata, data semuanya dari `data/*.ts`.

**Routing**: 7 halaman utama + 2 rute dinamis `/armada/[slug]` dan `/paket/[slug]` (sudah punya `generateStaticParams` + `dynamicParams = false`).

**Data**: S driver dari `data/vehicles.ts`, `data/packages.ts`, `data/testimonials.ts`, `data/faq.ts`, `data/services`. Setiap item punya `seo: SeoInfo` + `slug` unik di `lib/types.ts`.

**Styling**: Custom tokens di `app/globals.css` (`--font-jakarta`, `--bg-primary: #fafafa`, `--accent: #005691`, `--text-heading: #004a7c`). Tidak ada konfigurasi warna `primary` lewat Tailwind config.

**SEO**: Manual tapi terstruktur — `sitemap.ts` generate auto, `robots.ts` sederhana, metadata per-page di tiap file `page.tsx`, canonical diambil dari `lib/constants.ts` (`SITE_URL = https://mahessaholiday.my.id`).

**Deployment**: `output: "export"` di `next.config.ts`, semua gambar lokal. Setambah route data-driven → harus tambah `generateStaticParams` + `export const dynamicParams = false`, lalu `npm run build`.

**Konversi**: WhatsApp saja (`+62 895-3270-77214`), tidak ada payment form atau booking form.

**Perintah tersedia**: `npm run dev`, `npm run build`, `npm run start`, `npm run lint`.

Jangan lupa cek `npm run build` setelah menambah rute baru. Apakah ada yang ingin ditanya atau langsung mulai perubahan?

jadi yang sudah termasuk itu mobil, bbm, dan driver

dan juga di detail armada kan ada armada lain cocok buatmu dan menampilkan 3 mobil, nah tambah 1 lagi biar genap jadi 4 mobil 

lalu testimoni yang di mobile error kaya ngeblank putih dan kayanya itu terlalu panjang ke kanan karena saya liat ada teks panjang ke kanan, saya pengennya testinya jadi card display kaya yang armada dan paket di beranda jadi 1 card yg bisa di slide, tapi yang testi tuh saya maunya user gabisa slide tapi slide sendiri setiap 1 detik

Let me rewrite the whole TestimonialCarousel to be cleaner:

```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import TestimonialCard from "./TestimonialCard";
import { testimonials } from "@/data/testimonials";

const EASE = [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] as const;
const MOBILE_INTERVAL = 1000; // 1 second auto-advance on mobile
const DESKTOP_INTERVAL = 5000; // 5 seconds on desktop

function getItemsPerPage(width: number): number {
  if (width < 768) return 1;
  if (width < 1024) return 2;
  return 4;
}

export default function TestimonialCarousel() {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const [itemsPerPage, setItemsPerPage] = useState<number>(4);
  const [pageIndex, setPageIndex] = useState(0);
  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false);
  const intervalRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const isMobile = itemsPerPage === 1;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, testimonials.length - itemsPerPage + 1);

  // Update itemsPerPage on resize
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleResize = () => {
      const w = window.innerWidth;
      const next = getItemsPerPage(w);
      setItemsPerPage((prev) => {
        if (prev !== next) setPageIndex(0);
        return next;
      });
    };
    handleResize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", handleResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", handleResize);
  }, []);

  // Auto-scroll for both mobile and desktop (paused on hover for desktop)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (reduce) return;
    if (paused) return;
    const interval = isMobile ? MOBILE_INTERVAL : DESKTOP_INTERVAL;
    intervalRef.current = window.setInterval(() => {
      setPageIndex((p) => (p + 1) % totalPages);
    }, interval);
    return () => {
      if (intervalRef.current !== null) window.clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
    };
  }, [reduce, isMobile, paused, totalPages]);

  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {
    setPageIndex((p) => (p - 1 + totalPages) % totalPages);
  }, [totalPages]);

  const handleNext = useCallback(() => {
    setPageIndex((p) => (p + 1) % totalPages);
  }, [totalPages]);

  const handleDot = useCallback((i: number) => {
    setPageIndex(i);
  }, []);

  const translateX = `calc(-${pageIndex * (100 / itemsPerPage)}% - ${pageIndex * (16 / itemsPerPage)}px)`;

  return (
    <section
      className="border-y border-line bg-surface/60 py-16 md:py-24"
      aria-label="Testimoni pelanggan"
      onMouseEnter={() => setPaused(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => setPaused(false)}
    >
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1300px] px-5 sm:px-8 md:px-12">
        <motion.header
          initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
          whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.4 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: EASE }}
          className="mb-10 text-center md:mb-12"
        >
          <span className="mb-2 inline-block font-mono text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-primary">
            Testimoni
          </span>
          <h2 className="mb-3 text-2xl font-extrabold leading-[1.05] tracking-[-0.03em] text-heading sm:text-3xl md:text-4xl">
            Kata Mereka tentang{" "}
            <span className="text-accent">Mahessa</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="mx-auto max-w-xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-body-text md:text-base">
            Ribuan pelanggan telah mempercayai layanan kami untuk berbagai kebutuhan perjalanan.
          </p>
        </motion.header>

        <div className="relative">
          {/* Desktop nav buttons */}
          {!isMobile && (
            <>
              <button ... prev />
              <button ... next />
            </>
          )}

          <div className="overflow-hidden">
            <motion.div
              className="flex"
              animate={{ x: translateX }}
              transition={isMobile ? { duration: 0.4, ease: EASE } : { duration: 0.5, ease: EASE }}
            >
              {testimonials.map((t, i) => (
                <div
                  key={t.id}
                  className="shrink-0 px-2"
                  style={{ width: `${100 / itemsPerPage}%` }}
                >
                  <TestimonialCard
                    name={t.name}
                    role={t.role}
                    quote={t.quote}
                    rating={t.rating}
                    index={i}
                  />
                </div>
              ))}
            </motion.div>
          </div>

          {/* Pagination dots */}
          <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
            {Array.from({ length: totalPages }).map((_, i) => {
              const isActive = i === pageIndex;
              return (
                <button key={i} type="button" onClick={() => handleDot(i)} ...>
                  ...
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```bash
git push origin main
```
